Geekz

DarthMader

(Employee) posted an Article

Edited July 7, 2025 at 11:54 AM

Geekz

Hey Folks, E

ngineers, Technicians and Specialists who are passionate about cybersecurity.

What #Geekz is all about:

Enhance Technical Skills & Knowledge:

Participants can explore how get better and deep insights, delve into modern security with real-world scenarios through interactive experiences

Exchange Ideas & Connect with Peers:

provides a platform to exchange firsthand experiences, discuss top-of-mind business topics, and connect with like-minded professionals from their industry/region who share their passion

Hands-on Exploration & Collaboration:

Attendees can engage in interactive sessions to dissect vulnerabilities, test attack paths, and collaborate in real-time to fortify defenses.

Stay Ahead of the Curve:

Discussions on critical aspects of cybersecurity and practical solutions, help attendees prepare for the future of technology.

Networking & Community Building:

The event fosters a strong cybersecurity community by bringing together peers, experts, and industry leaders, allowing for valuable contributions and discussions in a relaxed environment.

“Any intelligent fool can make things bigger and more complex… It takes a touch of genius and a lot of courage to move in the opposite direction.”

Use this space to post about nerdy stuff and share interessting topics

Community Highlights

Ben_Garrison

(Employee) posted an Article

October 13, 2025 at 5:35 PM

New Activity Feed

I am happy to announce the new Activity Feed that you notice on our homepage. This is designed to help you find content much easier.

Earlier this year, we created new custom content types. Such as blogs, articles and guides. These new types improved our content offering from just "questions". But then we were left with the need to have a way to find these new pieces of content and that's where the new activity feed comes in.

You can filter by content type and then sort by latest or most recently commented.  That's been a big ask, as I know it's sometimes difficult to see which conversations are ongoing.  We also included new icons next to the topic to help with identifying what types of content the thread is and also if it includes images or videos!

Overall just an awesome way to help find content and stay engaged.

Please let us know if you have any suggestions or feedback!

## How can I get a “Top Users” report for GenAI/ML cloud apps?

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/Articles/aSmPJ00000GeHc10AF/how-can-i-get-a-top-users-report-for-genaiml-cloud-apps
- Type: Article
- Last activity: 2026-02-18T16:45:05.000Z
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。

Article Details

Technical Guides

Ben_Garrison

(Employee) posted an Article

Edited February 18, 2026 at 4:45 PM

How can I get a “Top Users” report for GenAI/ML cloud apps?

With special thanks to

Tatiana Porras

- Sales Engineer, Solutions Center for their contributions to this guide.

Q: How can I get a “Top Users” report for GenAI/ML cloud apps (like ChatGPT or Microsoft Copilot)?

Many teams want to identify which users are most actively using GenAI/ML applications such as Microsoft Copilot or ChatGPT. You can build this using Interactive Reports and then schedule it to run monthly.

Below is one example approach. If you do something different in your environment, please share your method in the comments so others can compare.

1. Create a custom “Top Users” web report

Go to:

Analytics → Interactive Reports → Custom Reports

Click

New Report

(top right corner).

Set

Report Data Class

to:

Web.

Set the

Time Range

to the

Previous Month

Keeping this consistent makes scheduling and month-over-month comparison easier.

Enter a

Report Name

(for example:

Top Users – GenAI Apps

).

2. Add a widget for Microsoft Copilot

Click Add Widget. A Data Filters dialog will open.

Set:

Widget Name: e.g., Top Users – Microsoft Copilot

Data Type:

Use

Top:

Top 100

(or fewer, depending on your needs)

Units: Transactions or Bytes

Use

Transactions

if you care about

frequency of use.

Use

Bytes

if you care about

volume of data.

Chart Type: Bar

Add two filters:

Policy Action = Allow

This ensures you are only looking at users whose traffic is allowed.

Cloud Application = Microsoft Copilot

Click

OK

to save the widget.

3. Add a widget for ChatGPT (and other GenAI apps)

To include ChatGPT in the same report:

Click Add Widget again.

Set:

Widget Name:

e.g., Top Users – ChatGPT

Data Type

User

Top

: same value you used before (e.g., Top 100)

Units

: same as previous widget (to keep results comparable)

Chart Type

Bar

Add the same two filters, now for ChatGPT:

Policy Action

Allow

loud Application

ChatGPT

Click OK

Repeat these steps for any additional GenAI/ML applications you want to track. You can either:

Add multiple widgets in one consolidated report, or

Create separate reports per application (e.g., Top Users – GitHub Copilot, Top Users – Gemini, etc.).

Click Save

(top right corner) to save the custom report.

4. View and validate the custom report

Go to:

Analytics → Interactive Reports → Custom Reports

Locate your report and click View.

You should now see the “Top Users” bar charts for each GenAI application you configured.

In a production tenant, the list will typically include more users and a wider spread of consumption patterns.

Consider checking:

Are there unexpected power users?

Are there departments or roles using GenAI more heavily than anticipated?

Does the usage align with your acceptable use policy?

5. Schedule the report to run monthly

To make this useful over time, schedule the report and have it emailed to relevant stakeholders (e.g., Security, IT Ops, Compliance):

Go to:

Analytics → Interactive Reports → Scheduled Reports

Click Add.

Configure:

Schedule Name:

e.g.,

Monthly – Top GenAI Users

Custom Report:

choose the report you created above from the dropdown.

Recipients

: add one or more email addresses (mailing list recommended).

Set the schedule:

Frequency:

Monthly

Run on

1st day of the month

(or your preferred date)

Time zone

: match your primary operations / reporting timezone.

Click

Save.

Activate changes

if your environment requires it for scheduled reports.

For more details about scheduling, see the (internal or external) “

Scheduling Reports

” reference for your Zscaler product.

6. Important notes and limitations

As of Dec 2025, this specific “Top Users” report configuration is not automatically provided by a Gen AI Security subscription.

It must be set up as a Custom Web Report and then scheduled as described above.

Feature names and availability can vary by cloud, tenant, and license level, so always verify in your own UI.

Do you prefer “Top Users by Transactions” or “by Bytes” for GenAI, and why

Training Events

Deepti Gupta

(Employee) posted a Blog

August 1, 2025 at 7:36 PM

Meet your mentor and ask questions live!

Meet your mentor!

Supratik Khandelwal

will lead our very first Instructor Office Hours session this Friday.

With 18+ years across IT infrastructure, cybersecurity, data protection & virtualization— He brings deep experience and unmatched clarity to every session. He’s trained thousands globally and is known for turning complexity into confidence.

This Friday, Aug 04, he’s live to answer your questions like:

✅ “What training path fits my current role?”

✅ “What’s the difference between ZDTA and ZDTE?”

✅ “Where do I start if I’m new to Zscaler?”

🗓️ August 4 | 5:30 PM - 6:00 PM IST

Register now →

## Automated Workaround Script for RHEL 9 Boot Issue

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/Guides/aSoPJ0000006WLN0A2/automated-workaround-script-for-rhel-9-boot-issue
- Type: Guide
- Last activity: 2026-07-20T13:46:12.000Z
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。

Guide Details

ZPA - Forwarding

Sanjog

(Employee) posted a Guide

Edited 11h ago

Automated Workaround Script for RHEL 9 Boot Issue

Introduction

A recent Red Hat update (RHBA-2026:18665, released for RHEL 9.x on May 19, 2026) increased the size of a core system boot file (the initramfs image). On affected components, this larger file can exceed the space available in the boot partition, which may prevent the component from starting up after an operating system update.

We strongly recommend applying the workaround below before performing any OS updates to prevent disruption.

For full details on the underlying issue, affected components, and diagnostic guidance, refer to the Trust post:

https://trust.zscaler.com/private.zscaler.com/posts/30441

This guide provides instructions for applying the temporary workaround using a script.

Scripted Workaround

(Link to download the script)

Zscaler provides a script that applies the workaround automatically. It includes built-in pre-flight checks, idempotent configuration handling, and full logging to /var/log/disable-drm-dracut.log for audit and troubleshooting purposes.

Step 1 — Copy the script to the affected instance

(for example, to the /tmp directory):

scp

disable-drm-dracut.sh

@:/tmp/

If inbound file transfer (SCP/SFTP) is not permitted to the instance, you can instead create the file directly over an SSH session. Run the following command, paste the full contents of the script, and then type SCRIPT_EOF on a new line to finish:

cat > /tmp/disable-drm-dracut.sh << 'SCRIPT_EOF'

# paste the script contents here

SCRIPT_EOF

Step 2 — Execute the script with elevated privileges:

sudo bash /tmp/disable-drm-dracut.sh

Note: Invoke the script via sudo bash as shown above. Executing it directly (e.g., sudo /tmp/disable-drm-dracut.sh) may fail with a Permission denied error on hardened systems where /tmp is mounted with the noexec option.

Step 3 — Verify successful completion by checking the exit code:

echo $?

An exit code of 0 indicates success. Any non-zero exit code indicates a failure; refer to /var/log/disable-drm-dracut.log for details.

Step 4 — Reboot the instance for the change to take effect:

sudo reboot

Step 5 — Once the instance is back online and healthy, proceed with the operating system update:

sudo yum update -y

We recommend performing this action during a planned maintenance window and validating the workaround on a single system before applying it broadly across your environment.

Associated Tags

automation

## [VIDEO] Community Author Spotlight - Zero Trust Architecture

[VIDEO] Community Author Spotlight - Zero Trust Architecture

Authors

@Nathan_Howe

@sganguli

and

@gfesta

step into our Community Spotlight to discuss the book “The Seven Elements of Highly Successful Zero Trust Architecture?.

You can find more information on the book on our

Zscaler site

. To request an ebook download, please click on this

download link

Zscaler is thrilled to announce the

Zscaler Zero Trust Certified Architect (ZTCA)

certification, the industry’s first comprehensive Zero Trust certification. The first 25 folks to drop us a comment on our Community post will get the special discount code to get your certification! Become a Zero Trust Architect today!

The Seven Elements of Highly Successful Zero Trust Architecture

walks you through how to build your own security architecture by unlocking the power of the world’s largest security cloud to meet zero trust initiatives. If you’re a network and security professional that oversees zero-trust initiatives, this needs to be on your reading short list.

## Get prepared and earn your ZDTA Certification!

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u0000AOCsLxCQL/get-prepared-and-earn-your-zdta-certification
- Type: Q&A
- Last activity: 2024-03-25T19:45:32.000Z

Certification

Katie Schmidt

(Employee) asked a question.

March 19, 2024 at 7:40 PM

Get prepared and earn your ZDTA Certification!

We have had a lot of people earning their

ZDTA certification

and we want to be sure you are fully prepared and successful in earning this industry-leading certification!

First things first, make sure you complete the required Pre-Reqs: Zscaler for Users-Essentials e-learning and the Zscaler for Users-Essentials Labs (get signed up via

Zscaler Academy

if you have not done this already). Our ZDTA exam is a 90 minute, 50 question online, proctored exam.

We highly recommend you review the

ZDTA study guide

as it is a valuable resource in ensuring you are fully prepared for success!

Certification

## Zscaler bulk emails limitation issue

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u0000Ac5LBmCQM/zscaler-bulk-emails-limitation-issue
- Type: Q&A
- Last activity: 2024-09-19T18:40:32.000Z
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。

ZUG - India

rohinr

(Partner) asked a question.

September 17, 2024 at 6:16 AM

Zscaler bulk emails limitation issue

Hello, we are facing an issue while sending bulk emails to users of our organisation. Just curious if Zscaler has any such limitations like max 255 recipients. If yes, then how to resolve the problem.

ZUG - India

2 answers

263 views

Ramesh Mani

(Partner)

2 years ago

See here

https://help.zscaler.com/unified/ranges-limitations

manuel

(Customer)

2 years ago

Hey Rohinr,

maybe I got your question wrong, but I see no correlation between Zscaler and your email-issue. Zscaler does not scan emails out-of-the-box and  has no limitations in terms of "number of recipients" of emails. Thats two complete different topics. I would rather start looking at your mail-service provider here.

This

could

be another story if you use "Outbound-Email-DLP", but I have no knowledge here and I also think thats not what you are talking about.

BR

Manuel

## Request time out error in office laptop with zscaler installed

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D5PJ00000evaXs0AI/request-time-out-error-in-office-laptop-with-zscaler-installed
- Type: Q&A
- Last activity: 2025-10-07T04:02:05.000Z
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。

ZUG - India

ashoknegi86

(Customer) asked a question.

Edited September 17, 2025 at 12:59 PM

Request time out error in office laptop with zscaler installed

Hi Team, This is my company laptop(Thinkpad). Zscaler is installed in this laptop. this laptop is working fine in company network also working fine with mobile network.. there is not a single packet drop( request time out ) error while pinging any website.

But soon as i am connecting it to my home wifi, i am getting frequent request time out error. but my personal laptop is working fine there is not a single request time out. if i disable zscaler in my office laptop then it is working fine.

Please let me know how to fix this.. company is saying this this is not there issue and ISP provide is saying this is not there issue.

ZUG - India

4 answers

507 views

ashoknegi86

(Customer)

10 months ago

Please let me know if there is any solution for this.

Ramesh Mani

(Partner)

10 months ago

Initiate a detailed troubleshooting with your IT person, to identify and fix this issue. This would need a troubleshooting session with wireshark logs.

Sachin Chauhan

(Partner)

10 months ago

Most of the cases are related to ISP only. Connectivity issue between your home ISP to Zscaler cloud. As Mani mentioned. you need to ask your IT person for a debugging and provide evidence to your ISP.

Arun

(Partner)

9 months ago

check which dc is connecting before and after connecting to your home wifi.sometimes it will differes with your ISP location that will leads to connects to different dc.
